	 $(function(){
         $("#login_user_123").click(function(e){
          
             e.preventDefault();
             var login_err_count = 0;
               $(".validloginform .input-required1").each(function(){ 
                 var value = $.trim($(this).val());  
               
                  if(value==""){ 
             $('.'+$(this).attr('tag')).html($(this).attr('name') + ' required');
             login_err_count = 1;
            }else{
             if($(this).attr('type')=="email"){
                if( !isEmail123(value)){ 
                     $('.'+$(this).attr('tag')).html('valid email required');
                     login_err_count = 1;
                }

                $('.'+$(this).attr('tag')).html('');
             }
            }
 }) 

        if(login_err_count==0){
           $("#suff_button123").trigger('click');
        }
            });
     })

     function isEmail123(email) {

  var email_validation_regexp = /^[a-z0-9!#$%&'*+\/=?^_`{|}~-]+(?:\.[a-z0-9!#$%&'*+\/=?^_`{|}~-]+)*@(?:[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]*[a-z0-9])?\.)+[a-z](?:[a-z0-9-]*[a-z0-9])$/gi
   if (email_validation_regexp.test(email)  ) {
      return true;
   }else{
      return false;
   }
}

<script>
    function openNavcommon() {
        //document.getElementById("myNav").style.width = "100%";
        $("#myNav").show();
    }

    function closeNavcommon() {
        $("#myNav").hide();
    }

     function openNavcommonpop(divid) { 
 
        document.getElementById(divid).style.display = "block";

    }

    function closeNavcommonpop(divid) {
       // document.getElementById(divid).style.width = "0%";
        //$("#"+div).hide();
          document.getElementById(divid).style.display = "none";
    }


  


     function deletecarfromcookie(VehicleID){
     deleteselectedcars(VehicleID);
 }

   function deleteselectedcars(VehicleID){
    var mygaragecar = "";
    openNavcommon();
       $.post('admin/shop_by_tab.php?userid=0&mode=garagedelete&VehicleID='+VehicleID)
       .done(function(data){  
       if(VehicleID==mygaragecar){
           var siteurlredirect = $("#siteurlredirect").val();
 window.location.href = siteurlredirect+"home.php";
       }else{
         location.reload();
       }
       
         
       })
    }

    closeNavcommon();
     location.reload();
          /*data =  $.parseJSON(data); //alert(data.toSource())
           $("#mycomparelists").html(data.replace_section);
            $(".pcount").html(data.total_count);

           (".pamount1").html(data.price1);
           (".pamount2").html(data.price2);*/
   })
  // setTimeout(function(){ location.reload(); }, 100);

}

$(".tirecombination").click(function(){
openNavcommonpop('popupexample');
var refreshId =setInterval(function() {
              var customer_response = $(this).attr('data');  
  
      //  alert(default_size);
      closeNavcommonpop('popupexample');
      var selectedvehicle = ''; 
      var vehicleid = '';
      var staggered_package = '';
      var productincart1 = "";
      var productincart2 = ""; 
      if(selectedproductid!=""){
          productincart1 = "&selectedproductid1="+selectedproductid;
      }

      if($.trim(selectedvehicle)!=""){
        var res = selectedvehicle.split("=>");  
        if ($("#checkbox_ft_2").length > 0){
                if($("#checkbox_ft_2"). prop("checked") == true){
            var staggered_package = '&staggered_package=yes'; 
              }
        }
        openNavcommonpop('popupexample');
        redirect_url = 'tiresbyvehicles.php?vehicleid='+staggered_package+'&stat=wheel-tire-package&selectedsize='+default_size+productincart1;
   var siteurlredirect = $("#siteurlredirect").val();
        window.location.href=siteurlredirect+redirect_url;
      }else{
        alert("Please select a vehicle to proceed");
      }
      
    
           clearInterval(refreshId);
        }, 5000);
  })

                if($("#checkbox_ft_2"). prop("checked") == true){
            var staggered_package = '&staggered_package=yes'; 
              }
        }

        if(default_size2!=""){
            var staggered_package = '&staggered_package=yes'; 
        }

         var productincart1 = "";
      var productincart2 = ""; 
      if(selectedproductid!=""){
          productincart1 = "&selectedproductid1="+selectedproductid;

       if(selectedproductid2!=""){

       openNavcommonpop('popupexample4');
        redirect_url = 'tiresbyvehicles.php?vehicleid='+staggered_package+'&stat=wheel-tire-package&selectedsize='+default_size+productincart1+'&selectedsize2='+default_size2+productincart2;

  })

  $(".wheelcombination").click(function(){
      var customer_response = $(this).attr('data');
      if(customer_response=="no"){
           var siteurlredirect = $("#siteurlredirect").val();
        window.location.href=siteurlredirect+'mycart.php';
      }else{
      var selectedvehicle = '';
      if($.trim(selectedvehicle)!=""){
        var res = selectedvehicle.split("=>"); 
        redirect_url = 'wheelsbyvehicles.php?year='+res[0]+'&make='+res[1]+'&model='+res[2]+'&body='+res[3]+'&trim='+res[4]+'&tag=tire&page_l=1';
           var siteurlredirect = $("#siteurlredirect").val();
        window.location.href=siteurlredirect+redirect_url;
      }
      
      }
  })


})





    function sampleshopproduct(){
       var userselectmake = localStorage.getItem("userselectmake");
       if (null != localStorage.getItem("userselectyear")) {
    var userselectyear = localStorage.getItem("userselectyear");
     if ( $(".shop_by_year_wheel option[value='"+userselectyear+"']").length != 0 ){ 
             $(".shop_by_year_wheel").val(userselectyear);
              $("#shop_by_year").val(userselectyear);
            }
    $.post("admin/shop_by_tab.php?mode=year&year=" + userselectyear).done(function(a) {
        $("#shop_by_make , .shop_by_make_wheel").html(a);
        $("#shop_by_model , .shop_by_model_wheel").html('<option selected="" value="0">'+languagehelper["ln_model_select"]+'</option>');
        $("#shop_by_body ,.shop_by_body_wheel").html('<option selected="" value="0">'+languagehelper["ln_body_select"]+'</option>');
        $("#shop_by_trim ,.shop_by_trim_wheel").html('<option selected="" value="0">'+languagehelper["ln_trim_select"]+'</option>');
        if (null != localStorage.getItem("userselectmake")) {
            var b = localStorage.getItem("userselectmake"); 
              if ( $(".shop_by_make_wheel option[value='"+b+"']").length != 0 ){
              $("#shop_by_make , .shop_by_make_wheel").val(b);
            }
            $.post("admin/shop_by_tab.php?mode=make&year=" + userselectyear + "&make=" + b).done(function(a) {
                $("#shop_by_model , .shop_by_model_wheel").html(a);
                $("#shop_by_body ,.shop_by_body_wheel").html('<option selected="" value="0">'+languagehelper["ln_body_select"]+'</option>');
                $("#shop_by_trim ,.shop_by_trim_wheel").html('<option selected="" value="0">'+languagehelper["ln_trim_select"]+'</option>');
                if (null != localStorage.getItem("userselectmodel")) {
                    var c = localStorage.getItem("userselectmodel");
   if ( $(".shop_by_model_wheel option[value='"+c+"']").length != 0 ){ 
                    $("#shop_by_model , .shop_by_model_wheel").val(c);
                    }
                    $.post("admin/shop_by_tab.php?mode=model&year=" + userselectyear + "&make=" + b + "&model=" + c).done(function(a) {
                   $("#shop_by_body , .shop_by_body_wheel").html(a); 
/*if ( $("#shop_by_body option[value='"+a+"']").length != 0 ){
                        $("#shop_by_body , .shop_by_body_wheel").html(a); 
                        $("#shop_by_trim").html('<option selected="" value="0">'+languagehelper["ln_trim_select"]+'</option>');
                          }*/
                        if (null != localStorage.getItem("userselectbody")) {
                            var d = localStorage.getItem("userselectbody"); 
                              if ( $(".shop_by_body_wheel option[value='"+d+"']").length != 0 ){ 

                                      $("#shop_by_body , .shop_by_body_wheel").val(d);
                                     }
                          
                            $.post("admin/shop_by_tab.php?mode=body&year=" + userselectyear + "&make=" + b + "&model=" + c + "&body=" + d).done(function(a) {  
$("#shop_by_trim , .shop_by_trim_wheel").html(a);
/*if ( $("#shop_by_model option[value='"+a+"']").length != 0 ){
                                $("#shop_by_trim , .shop_by_trim_wheel").html(a);
                              }*/ 
                                if (null != localStorage.getItem("userselecttrim")) { 
                                    var b = localStorage.getItem("userselecttrim");  
                                      if ( $(".shop_by_trim_wheel option[value='"+b+"']").length != 0 ){ 

                                    $("#shop_by_trim , .shop_by_trim_wheel").val(b);
                                     }
                                }
                            });
                        }
                    });
                }
            });
        }
    });
}
    }

        window.location.href=siteurlredirect+"mycart.php"; 
     });
     
}

<script type="text/javascript">


$(function(){

   var options = {
                $FillMode: 2,                                  
                $AutoPlay: true,                                  
                $Idle: 4000,                            
                $PauseOnHover: 1,                               
                $ArrowKeyNavigation: true,                   
                $SlideEasing: $JssorEasing$.$EaseOutQuint,        
                $SlideDuration: 800,                             
                $MinDragOffsetToSlide: 20,                      
                $SlideSpacing: 0,                       
                $Cols: 1,                                
                $ParkingPosition: 0,                                
                $UISearchMode: 1,                                   
                $PlayOrientation: 1,                               
                $DragOrientation: 0,                               
                $BulletNavigatorOptions: {                          
                    $Class: $JssorBulletNavigator$,                
                    $ChanceToShow: 2,                            
                    $AutoCenter: 1,                                
                    $Steps: 1,                                      
                    $Rows: 1,                                      
                    $SpacingX: 8,                                  
                    $SpacingY: 8,                                  
                    $Orientation: 1,                               
                    $Scale: false                                  
                },

                $ArrowNavigatorOptions: {                          
                    $Class: $JssorArrowNavigator$,                 
                    $ChanceToShow: 2,                               
                    $AutoCenter: 2,                                 
                    $Steps: 1                                      
                }
            };
            var jssor_slider1 = new $JssorSlider$("slider1_container", options);

           function ScaleSlider() { 
                var bodyWidth = document.body.clientWidth;
                if (bodyWidth)
                    jssor_slider1.$ScaleWidth(Math.min(bodyWidth, 1920));
                else
                    window.setTimeout(ScaleSlider, 30);
            }
            ScaleSlider();
   $(window).bind("load", ScaleSlider);
            $(window).bind("resize", ScaleSlider);
            $(window).bind("orientationchange", ScaleSlider);
})

</script>
